========================================================
  BSHH Attack S3 — Malicious Controller, No RSU        
========================================================

  t=5.000   ①  V0 <-> V1: Normal V2V heartbeat exchange
  t=5.000   ②  V0/V1 -> Controller: legitimate HBs sent directly (no RSU)
  t=5.010   ③  Controller: legitimate HBs ACCEPTED, liveness table updated
  t=5.100   ④  Malicious Controller stores stale copies for internal replay
  t=10.000  ⑤  Controller internally replays stale HBs (no external packet)
  t=10.000  ⑥  Controller holds conflicting liveness -> faulty routing

  LOG FORMAT: controllers printed as Ctrl<idx>(ns3=<id>), vehicles as V<idx>(ns3=<id>)
  Every selected malicious controller executes STEP ④, ⑤, ⑥.

========================================
  PAIR: ATTACKER=Ctrl0(ns3=0)  VICTIMS=V40(ns3=45)/V147(ns3=152)
========================================
[t=5.000]  STEP ①  NORMAL V2V HEARTBEAT EXCHANGE
  V40(ns3=45) -> V147(ns3=152) : Heartbeat(Sender=V40(ns3=45), beacon_sending_time=5.000)
  V147(ns3=152) -> V40(ns3=45) : Heartbeat(Sender=V147(ns3=152), beacon_sending_time=5.000)

[t=5.000]  STEP ②  VEHICLES SEND HBs DIRECTLY TO CONTROLLER (no RSU)
  V40(ns3=45) -> Ctrl0(ns3=0) : Heartbeat(Sender=V40(ns3=45), beacon_sending_time=5.000)
  V147(ns3=152) -> Ctrl0(ns3=0) : Heartbeat(Sender=V147(ns3=152), beacon_sending_time=5.000)

[t=5.010]  STEP ③  CONTROLLER ACCEPTS LEGITIMATE HBs
  Ctrl0(ns3=0) liveness table updated:
    V40(ns3=45) : beacon_sending_time=5.000  ACCEPTED
    V147(ns3=152) : beacon_sending_time=5.000  ACCEPTED

[t=5.100]  STEP ④  MALICIOUS CONTROLLER STORES STALE COPIES
  Attacker Controller : Ctrl0(ns3=0)
  captured_packet for V40(ns3=45) : Heartbeat(Sender=V40(ns3=45), beacon_sending_time=5.000)  (from STEP ② legitimate exchange)
  captured_packet for V147(ns3=152) : Heartbeat(Sender=V147(ns3=152), beacon_sending_time=5.000)  (from STEP ② legitimate exchange)
  Action : Ctrl0(ns3=0) stores both stale copies for internal replay (STEP ⑤)

[t=7.000]  STEP ⑤  CONTROLLER INTERNAL REPLAY (no external packet)
  Attacker Controller : Ctrl0(ns3=0)
  Packet source       : stored STEP ④ stale copies
  Overwrites V40(ns3=45) liveness with V40(ns3=45)'s own stale beacon_sending_time=5.000 (correctly attributed, just old)
  Overwrites V147(ns3=152) liveness SLOT with a FORGED heartbeat
    physical_sender (true slot owner) = V147(ns3=152)
    claimed_sender  (forged identity)  = V40(ns3=45)
    Ctrl0(ns3=0) topology DB[V147(ns3=152)] <- Heartbeat(Sender=V40(ns3=45), beacon_sending_time=5.000)  IMPERSONATING V40(ns3=45)
  Different claimed vs. slot identity -> impersonation evidence
  No external packet sent — attack is entirely internal to Ctrl0(ns3=0)

[t=7.000]  STEP ⑥  FAULTY ROUTING CONSEQUENCES
  Ctrl0(ns3=0) now holds conflicting liveness:
    V40(ns3=45) : beacon_sending_time=5.000 < current t=7.000  [STALE]
    V147(ns3=152) : beacon_sending_time=5.000 < current t=7.000  [STALE/FORGED — claims to be V40(ns3=45)]
  Two liveness entries now assert the same identity (V40(ns3=45)) under different slots
  Packets may be routed using stale/non-existent liveness
  Expected impact: packet loss, added delay, degraded PDR
  <- ATTACK SUCCESS (no external packet)
